# Catalog attribution

Numerical audio embeddings derived from the available excerpts and windows recorded in tracks.json. No raw audio is distributed. Recording terms below remain applicable to derived representations. The application's MIT software license does not relicense this catalog.

## Model

LAION CLAP; Yusong Wu, Ke Chen, Tianyu Zhang, Yuchen Hui, Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick, Shlomo Dubnov. [laion/larger_clap_general](https://huggingface.co/laion/larger_clap_general/tree/ada0c23a36c4e8582805bb38fec3905903f18b41), [Apache 2.0](https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0). Weights are not distributed.

## Metadata

FMA: A Dataset for Music Analysis — Michaël Defferrard, Kirell Benzi, Pierre Vandergheynst and Xavier Bresson (ISMIR 2017). Metadata selected and normalized. — [Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/).
